  their  100644 57214e45226b4ad301db2ba774f8fb40f15907cd apps/backend/src/api/tenants/analytics/route.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,64 @@
+import type { AuthenticatedMedusaRequest, MedusaResponse } from "@medusajs/framework/http";
+import { TENANT_MODULE } from "../../../modules/tenant";
+import type TenantModuleService from "../../../modules/tenant/service";
+import { getTenantScope } from "../tenant-scope";
+import type { PutTenantAnalyticsBody } from "../validators";
+
+/**
+ * `GET`/`PUT /tenants/analytics` (t17-analytics) â€” the shop's own analytics
+ * counters, saved into `tenant.metadata.analytics` the same way
+ * `seo-verification` saves into `tenant.metadata.seo_verification`: merged
+ * with whatever else already lives in `metadata` so this route never clobbers
+ * a field it does not know about.
+ *
+ * Read by the storefront through `GET /storefront/by-host` (piece 9's public
+ * resolver), never through this authenticated route â€” a storefront page has
+ * no tenant session.
+ */
+
+type AnalyticsSettings = { yandex_metrica_id: string | null; ga4_measurement_id: string | null };
+
+const readAnalytics = (metadata: Record<string, unknown>): AnalyticsSettings => {
+  const current = (metadata.analytics ?? {}) as Partial<AnalyticsSettings>;
+  return {
+    yandex_metrica_id: current.yandex_metrica_id ?? null,
+    ga4_measurement_id: current.ga4_measurement_id ?? null,
+  };
+};
+
+export const GET = async (req: AuthenticatedMedusaRequest, res: MedusaResponse) => {
+  const scope = await getTenantScope(req);
+  const tenantModuleService: TenantModuleService = req.scope.resolve(TENANT_MODULE);
+  const tenant = await tenantModuleService.retrieveTenant(scope.id);
+  const metadata = (tenant.metadata ?? {}) as Record<string, unknown>;
+
+  res.json({ analytics: readAnalytics(metadata) });
+};
+
+export const PUT = async (
+  req: AuthenticatedMedusaRequest<PutTenantAnalyticsBody>,
+  res: MedusaResponse,
+) => {
+  const scope = await getTenantScope(req);
+  const tenantModuleService: TenantModuleService = req.scope.resolve(TENANT_MODULE);
+
+  const tenant = await tenantModuleService.retrieveTenant(scope.id);
+  const metadata = (tenant.metadata ?? {}) as Record<string, unknown>;
+  const current = readAnalytics(metadata);
+
+  const yandex_metrica_id =
+    req.validatedBody.yandex_metrica_id !== undefined
+      ? req.validatedBody.yandex_metrica_id || null
+      : current.yandex_metrica_id;
+  const ga4_measurement_id =
+    req.validatedBody.ga4_measurement_id !== undefined
+      ? req.validatedBody.ga4_measurement_id || null
+      : current.ga4_measurement_id;
+
+  const updated = await tenantModuleService.updateTenants({
+    id: scope.id,
+    metadata: { ...metadata, analytics: { yandex_metrica_id, ga4_measurement_id } },
+  });
+
+  res.json({ analytics: readAnalytics(updated.metadata as Record<string, unknown>) });
+};

  their  100644 c9a0420afaf88b7e4b02b77f4ddf1e658733c4ee apps/backend/src/api/tenants/analytics/summary/route.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,226 @@
+import type { AuthenticatedMedusaRequest, MedusaResponse } from "@medusajs/framework/http";
+import { ContainerRegistrationKeys } from "@medusajs/framework/utils";
+import type { Knex } from "@medusajs/framework/mikro-orm/knex";
+import { listTenantOrderIds } from "../../tenant-scope";
+
+/**
+ * `GET /tenants/analytics/summary` (t17-analytics, step 5) â€” revenue, order
+ * count and average order value for a period the owner picks, the change
+ * against the immediately preceding period of the same length, a daily
+ * breakdown and the five best-selling products. Computed here, over the
+ * tenant's own orders (`listTenantOrderIds`), never fetched whole into the
+ * panel.
+ *
+ * `order.total` is not a stored column â€” it lives in `order_summary.totals`,
+ * a JSON blob keyed `current_order_total`, the same fact
+ * `customers/helpers.ts` (`getTenantCustomerAggregates`) already measured
+ * against the live schema, so this route joins it the same way rather than
+ * calling `getOrdersListWorkflow` once per order.
+ *
+ * `has_orders` is `false` only when the tenant has never had a single order â€”
+ * the honest empty state (step 6: "Ğ·Ğ°ĞºĞ°Ğ·Ğ¾Ğ² Ğ¿Ğ¾ĞºĞ° Ğ½ĞµÑ‚", never zeroes that look
+ * like a loss). A tenant WITH orders whose chosen period happens to be empty
+ * still gets `has_orders: true` and a real, computed zero: that zero is data,
+ * not a stand-in for "not loaded yet" (rule 20a) â€” the panel's own loading
+ * state is a separate, third value the frontend tracks itself (rule 19h).
+ */
+
+type OrderRow = {
+  id: string;
+  created_at: Date;
+  total: number;
+  currency_code: string | null;
+};
+
+const DAY_MS = 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000;
+
+const startOfDay = (date: Date): Date =>
+  new Date(Date.UTC(date.getUTCFullYear(), date.getUTCMonth(), date.getUTCDate()));
+
+const parseDate = (value: unknown): Date | null => {
+  if (typeof value !== "string") {
+    return null;
+  }
+  const parsed = new Date(value);
+  return Number.isNaN(parsed.getTime()) ? null : parsed;
+};
+
+/**
+ * `{ start, end }` (end exclusive) for the requested period, plus the
+ * immediately preceding period of the same length, so "change against the
+ * previous period" always compares like-for-like durations.
+ */
+const resolveRange = (
+  req: AuthenticatedMedusaRequest,
+  now: Date,
+): { start: Date; end: Date; prevStart: Date; prevEnd: Date; key: string } => {
+  const period = typeof req.query.period === "string" ? req.query.period : "7d";
+  const today = startOfDay(now);
+  const end = new Date(today.getTime() + DAY_MS);
+
+  if (period === "custom") {
+    const from = parseDate(req.query.from);
+    const to = parseDate(req.query.to);
+
+    if (from && to && to.getTime() > from.getTime()) {
+      const start = startOfDay(from);
+      const rangeEnd = new Date(startOfDay(to).getTime() + DAY_MS);
+      const length = rangeEnd.getTime() - start.getTime();
+
+      return {
+        start,
+        end: rangeEnd,
+        prevStart: new Date(start.getTime() - length),
+        prevEnd: start,
+        key: "custom",
+      };
+    }
+    // Fall through to the 7-day default on a malformed custom range: the
+    // range is what the caller asked for, not a fact that can 400 the whole
+    // panel screen.
+  }
+
+  const days = period === "today" ? 1 : period === "30d" ? 30 : 7;
+  const start = new Date(end.getTime() - days * DAY_MS);
+
+  return {
+    start,
+    end,
+    prevStart: new Date(start.getTime() - days * DAY_MS),
+    prevEnd: start,
+    key: period === "today" || period === "30d" ? period : "7d",
+  };
+};
+
+const round2 = (value: number): number => Math.round(value * 100) / 100;
+
+const aggregate = (orders: OrderRow[]) => {
+  const revenue = round2(orders.reduce((sum, order) => sum + order.total, 0));
+  const order_count = orders.length;
+  const aov = order_count > 0 ? round2(revenue / order_count) : 0;
+  return { revenue, order_count, aov };
+};
+
+/** Percent change, or null when the previous period has nothing to compare against. */
+const changeOf = (current: number, previous: number): number | null => {
+  if (previous === 0) {
+    return null;
+  }
+  return round2(((current - previous) / previous) * 100);
+};
+
+export const GET = async (req: AuthenticatedMedusaRequest, res: MedusaResponse) => {
+  const ownedIds = await listTenantOrderIds(req);
+
+  if (ownedIds.length === 0) {
+    res.json({ has_orders: false });
+    return;
+  }
+
+  const pg = req.scope.resolve<Knex>(ContainerRegistrationKeys.PG_CONNECTION);
+  const now = new Date();
+  const { start, end, prevStart, prevEnd, key } = resolveRange(req, now);
+
+  const rawOrders: {
+    id: string;
+    created_at: Date;
+    total: string | null;
+    currency_code: string | null;
+  }[] = await pg({ o: "order" })
+    .leftJoin({ os: "order_summary" }, function joinSummary() {
+      this.on("os.order_id", "o.id").andOnNull("os.deleted_at");
+    })
+    .whereIn("o.id", ownedIds)
+    .whereNull("o.deleted_at")
+    .where("o.is_draft_order", false)
+    .select("o.id", "o.created_at", "o.currency_code")
+    .select(
+      pg.raw("coalesce((os.totals->>'current_order_total')::numeric, 0) as total"),
+    );
+
+  const orders: OrderRow[] = rawOrders.map((row) => ({
+    id: row.id,
+    created_at: new Date(row.created_at),
+    total: Number(row.total ?? 0),
+    currency_code: row.currency_code,
+  }));
+
+  const currency_code = orders[0]?.currency_code ?? null;
+
+  const inRange = (from: Date, to: Date) =>
+    orders.filter((order) => {
+      const at = order.created_at.getTime();
+      return at >= from.getTime() && at < to.getTime();
+    });
+
+  const currentOrders = inRange(start, end);
+  const previousOrders = inRange(prevStart, prevEnd);
+
+  const current = aggregate(currentOrders);
+  const previous = aggregate(previousOrders);
+
+  const daily: { date: string; revenue: number; order_count: number }[] = [];
+  for (let cursor = start.getTime(); cursor < end.getTime(); cursor += DAY_MS) {
+    const dayStart = new Date(cursor);
+    const dayEnd = new Date(cursor + DAY_MS);
+    const dayOrders = inRange(dayStart, dayEnd);
+    daily.push({
+      date: dayStart.toISOString().slice(0, 10),
+      revenue: aggregate(dayOrders).revenue,
+      order_count: dayOrders.length,
+    });
+  }
+
+  let top_products: { product_id: string; title: string; quantity: number; revenue: number }[] = [];
+
+  if (currentOrders.length > 0) {
+    const rows: {
+      product_id: string;
+      title: string;
+      quantity: string;
+      revenue: string;
+    }[] = await pg({ oi: "order_item" })
+      .join({ oli: "order_line_item" }, function joinLineItem() {
+        this.on("oli.id", "oi.item_id").andOnNull("oli.deleted_at");
+      })
+      .whereIn(
+        "oi.order_id",
+        currentOrders.map((order) => order.id),
+      )
+      .whereNull("oi.deleted_at")
+      .whereNotNull("oli.product_id")
+      .groupBy("oli.product_id", "oli.title")
+      .select({ product_id: "oli.product_id", title: "oli.title" })
+      .sum({ quantity: "oi.quantity" })
+      .sum({ revenue: pg.raw("oi.quantity * oli.unit_price") });
+
+    top_products = rows
+      .map((row) => ({
+        product_id: row.product_id,
+        title: row.title,
+        quantity: Number(row.quantity ?? 0),
+        revenue: round2(Number(row.revenue ?? 0)),
+      }))
+      .sort((a, b) => b.revenue - a.revenue)
+      .slice(0, 5);
+  }
+
+  res.json({
+    has_orders: true,
+    currency_code,
+    period: {
+      key,
+      from: start.toISOString(),
+      to: new Date(end.getTime() - 1).toISOString(),
+    },
+    current,
+    previous,
+    change: {
+      revenue_pct: changeOf(current.revenue, previous.revenue),
+      order_count_pct: changeOf(current.order_count, previous.order_count),
+      aov_pct: changeOf(current.aov, previous.aov),
+    },
+    daily,
+    top_products,
+  });
+};

+import type { MedusaContainer } from "@medusajs/framework";
+import type { IAuthModuleService, Logger } from "@medusajs/framework/types";
+import { ContainerRegistrationKeys, Modules } from "@medusajs/framework/utils";
+import type { Knex } from "@medusajs/framework/mikro-orm/knex";
+import { TENANT_MODULE } from "../modules/tenant";
+import TenantModuleService from "../modules/tenant/service";
+import createTenantWorkflow from "../workflows/tenant/create-tenant";
+import linkOrderToTenantWorkflow from "../workflows/tenant/link-order-to-tenant";
+
+/**
+ * t17-analytics: throwaway fixtures for measuring the analytics counters and
+ * sales summary on a local stand. Not wired into any pnpm script â€” run once
+ * with `npx medusa exec ./src/scripts/t17-seed-test-tenant.ts` against a
+ * scratch database, never against a real one. Same shape as
+ * `t13-seed-test-tenant.ts`.
+ *
+ * Two tenants:
+ *   - "t17shopa": has a Yandex Metrica id AND a GA4 id saved, three orders
+ *     today and two three days ago, with line items, for the panel's sales
+ *     summary and the storefront's "loads the script" check.
+ *   - "t17shopb": neither counter set, zero orders â€” the "loads nothing" and
+ *     "Ğ·Ğ°ĞºĞ°Ğ·Ğ¾Ğ² Ğ¿Ğ¾ĞºĞ° Ğ½ĞµÑ‚" checks.
+ */
+const PROVIDER = "emailpass";
+
+export default async function seedT17TestTenant({ container }: { container: MedusaContainer }) {
+  const logger = container.resolve(ContainerRegistrationKeys.LOGGER) as Logger;
+  const query = container.resolve(ContainerRegistrationKeys.QUERY);
+  const authModuleService: IAuthModuleService = container.resolve(Modules.AUTH);
+  const tenantModuleService: TenantModuleService = container.resolve(TENANT_MODULE);
+  const pg = container.resolve<Knex>(ContainerRegistrationKeys.PG_CONNECTION);
+  const password = "supersecret";
+
+  const ensureTenant = async (handle: string, name: string, adminEmail: string) => {
+    let [tenant] = await tenantModuleService.listTenants({ handle });
+
+    if (tenant) {
+      logger.info(`t17-seed: ${handle} already exists (${tenant.id})`);
+      return tenant;
+    }
+
+    const registered = await authModuleService.register(PROVIDER, {
+      body: { email: adminEmail, password },
+    });
+
+    if (!registered.success || !registered.authIdentity) {
+      throw new Error(`t17-seed: could not register ${adminEmail}: ${registered.error}`);
+    }
+
+    const { result } = await createTenantWorkflow(container).run({
+      input: {
+        name,
+        handle,
+        admin: { email: adminEmail, first_name: "Test", last_name: "Owner" },
+        authIdentityId: registered.authIdentity.id,
+      },
+    });
+
+    logger.info(`t17-seed: created ${handle} (${result.tenant.id}), key ${result.tenant.api_key.token}`);
+    return result.tenant as typeof tenant;
+  };
+
+  const shopA = await ensureTenant("t17shopa", "T17 Shop A", "admin@t17shopa.example");
+  const shopB = await ensureTenant("t17shopb", "T17 Shop B", "admin@t17shopb.example");
+
+  await tenantModuleService.updateTenants({
+    id: shopA.id,
+    metadata: {
+      ...(shopA.metadata as Record<string, unknown> | null),
+      analytics: { yandex_metrica_id: "12345678", ga4_measurement_id: "G-TESTID1234" },
+    },
+  });
+  logger.info("t17-seed: shopA analytics saved (yandex 12345678, ga4 G-TESTID1234)");
+
+  const {
+    data: [withChannel],
+  } = await query.graph({
+    entity: "tenant",
+    fields: ["id", "sales_channel.id"],
+    filters: { id: [shopA.id] },
+  });
+  const salesChannelId = withChannel?.sales_channel?.id as string | undefined;
+
+  if (!salesChannelId) {
+    throw new Error("t17-seed: shopA has no sales channel link.");
+  }
+
+  const [existingCustomer] = await pg("customer").where({ email: "buyer@t17shopa.example" }).select("id");
+  let customerId = existingCustomer?.id as string | undefined;
+
+  if (!customerId) {
+    customerId = `cus_t17${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 10)}`;
+    await pg("customer").insert({
+      id: customerId,
+      first_name: "Ğ¢ĞµÑÑ‚",
+      last_name: "ĞŸĞ¾ĞºÑƒĞ¿Ğ°Ñ‚ĞµĞ»ÑŒ",
+      email: "buyer@t17shopa.example",
+      has_account: false,
+      created_at: new Date(),
+      updated_at: new Date(),
+    });
+  }
+
+  const {
+    data: [existingProduct],
+  } = await query.graph({
+    entity: "product",
+    fields: ["id", "title"],
+    filters: {},
+    pagination: { take: 1 },
+  });
+
+  const productId = (existingProduct?.id as string | undefined) ?? null;
+  const productTitle = (existingProduct?.title as string | undefined) ?? "Test product";
+
+  const [existingOrders] = await pg("order").where({ email: "buyer@t17shopa.example" }).count("id as c");
+
+  if (Number(existingOrders?.c ?? 0) === 0) {
+    const plan = [
+      { daysAgo: 0, total: 2500 },
+      { daysAgo: 0, total: 1800 },
+      { daysAgo: 0, total: 3200 },
+      { daysAgo: 3, total: 1200 },
+      { daysAgo: 3, total: 900 },
+    ];
+
+    for (const [index, entry] of plan.entries()) {

+      const createdAt = new Date(Date.now() - entry.daysAgo * 86400000);
+

+        id: orderId,
+        customer_id: customerId,
+        sales_channel_id: salesChannelId,
+        currency_code: "rub",
+        status: "completed",
+        is_draft_order: false,
+        email: "buyer@t17shopa.example",
+        created_at: createdAt,
+        updated_at: createdAt,
+      });
+
+      await pg("order_summary").insert({
+        id: `ordsum_t17${index}${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 8)}`,
+        order_id: orderId,
+        totals: JSON.stringify({ current_order_total: entry.total }),
+        created_at: createdAt,
+        updated_at: createdAt,
+      });
+
+      if (productId) {
+        // `raw_*` mirrors the numeric column as the BigNumber JSON shape
+        // Medusa's own writers use (`{ value, precision }`) â€” NOT NULL on
+        // both tables, measured against the live schema after the first
+        // insert attempt here failed on it.
+        const rawAmount = (value: number) => JSON.stringify({ value: String(value), precision: 20 });
+        const lineItemId = `orli_t17${index}${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 8)}`;
+        await pg("order_line_item").insert({
+          id: lineItemId,
+          title: productTitle,
+          product_id: productId,
+          unit_price: entry.total,
+          raw_unit_price: rawAmount(entry.total),
+          requires_shipping: true,
+          is_discountable: true,
+          is_tax_inclusive: false,
+          created_at: createdAt,
+          updated_at: createdAt,
+        });
+        await pg("order_item").insert({
+          id: `oritem_t17${index}${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 8)}`,
+          order_id: orderId,
+          version: 1,
+          item_id: lineItemId,
+          quantity: 1,
+          raw_quantity: rawAmount(1),
+          fulfilled_quantity: 0,
+          raw_fulfilled_quantity: rawAmount(0),
+          shipped_quantity: 0,
+          raw_shipped_quantity: rawAmount(0),
+          return_requested_quantity: 0,
+          raw_return_requested_quantity: rawAmount(0),
+          return_received_quantity: 0,
+          raw_return_received_quantity: rawAmount(0),
+          return_dismissed_quantity: 0,
+          raw_return_dismissed_quantity: rawAmount(0),
+          written_off_quantity: 0,
+          raw_written_off_quantity: rawAmount(0),
+          delivered_quantity: 0,
+          raw_delivered_quantity: rawAmount(0),
+          unit_price: entry.total,
+          raw_unit_price: rawAmount(entry.total),
+          created_at: createdAt,
+          updated_at: createdAt,
+        });
+      }
+
+      await linkOrderToTenantWorkflow(container).run({ input: { order_id: orderId } });
+    }
+
+    logger.info(`t17-seed: shopA got ${plan.length} orders`);
+  } else {
+    logger.info("t17-seed: shopA already has orders, skipping");
+  }
+
+  logger.info(`t17-seed: shopB (${shopB.id}) has no analytics and no orders â€” control fixture`);

@@ -0,0 +1,57 @@
+import "server-only"
+import { decodeTenantHeaderValue } from "@lib/tenant/headers"
+import { headers as nextHeaders } from "next/headers"
+import { GA_MEASUREMENT_ID } from "@lib/util/env"
+
+export type AnalyticsConfig = {
+  yandexMetricaId: string | null
+  ga4MeasurementId: string | null
+}
+
+/**
+ * This request's analytics ids (t17-analytics step 1), carried by the
+ * middleware on `x-tenant-analytics` the same way the search-console tags
+ * travel (`lib/data/seo-verification.ts`).
+ *
+ * A resolved tenant (the header is present, even if both ids inside it are
+ * null â€” see below) NEVER falls back to `NEXT_PUBLIC_GA_MEASUREMENT_ID`: that
+ * variable is the single-store install's own fallback, one id for the whole
+ * platform, and applying it to a tenant would count every shop with no
+ * counter of its own under one owner's GA property. Only the absence of a
+ * tenant at all (single-store mode, no `x-tenant-*` headers set) reads the
+ * build-time id.
+ *
+ * The middleware only ever sets the header when at least one id is present
+ * (`tenant.analytics` is null otherwise), so the header's mere presence
+ * already means "this is a resolved, multi-tenant request."
+ */
+export const getTenantAnalytics = async (): Promise<AnalyticsConfig> => {
+  try {
+    const requestHeaders = await nextHeaders()
+    const raw = decodeTenantHeaderValue(requestHeaders.get("x-tenant-analytics"))
+
+    if (!raw) {
+      // No tenant resolved this host (or it set neither counter): a resolved
+      // tenant with a `x-tenant-handle` but no analytics header means it saved
+      // no ids and gets no analytics at all (step 1); an UNRESOLVED host
+      // (single-store mode) gets the build-time fallback.
+      const handle = decodeTenantHeaderValue(requestHeaders.get("x-tenant-handle"))
+      if (handle) {
+        return { yandexMetricaId: null, ga4MeasurementId: null }
+      }
+      return { yandexMetricaId: null, ga4MeasurementId: GA_MEASUREMENT_ID ?? null }
+    }
+
+    const parsed = JSON.parse(raw) as Partial<{
+      yandexMetricaId: string | null
+      ga4MeasurementId: string | null
+    }>
+
+    return {
+      yandexMetricaId: parsed.yandexMetricaId ?? null,
+      ga4MeasurementId: parsed.ga4MeasurementId ?? null,
+    }
+  } catch {
+    return { yandexMetricaId: null, ga4MeasurementId: null }
+  }
+}

  their  100644 8a024b7bb7c962027ef03a11c00dd4e1afebc975 apps/storefront/src/modules/common/components/analytics-consent/consent.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,47 @@
+"use client"
+
+/**
+ * Analytics consent (t17-analytics step 3): this storefront had no consent
+ * gate at all before this task (measured â€” `README.md` documented it as a
+ * known gap: "There is no consent gate. The tag loads for everyone once the
+ * ID is set."). This is the state, stored per browser in `localStorage` so it
+ * survives a reload without a cookie round trip to the server.
+ *
+ * "unknown" (nobody has answered yet) is its own state, not a silent "denied"
+ * â€” the banner renders only for it, and neither analytics script mounts
+ * before it turns into "granted" (rule 19h: a third state must not collapse
+ * into a boolean).
+ */
+
+export type ConsentState = "unknown" | "granted" | "denied"
+
+const STORAGE_KEY = "medusa_analytics_consent"
+const CHANGE_EVENT = "medusa-analytics-consent-change"
+
+export const readConsent = (): ConsentState => {
+  if (typeof window === "undefined") {
+    return "unknown"
+  }
+
+  try {
+    const value = window.localStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEY)
+    return value === "granted" || value === "denied" ? value : "unknown"
+  } catch {
+    // Private browsing / storage disabled: treat every visit as "unknown" and
+    // keep asking, rather than silently granting analytics nobody agreed to.
+    return "unknown"
+  }
+}
+
+export const writeConsent = (value: "granted" | "denied"): void => {
+  try {
+    window.localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEY, value)
+  } catch {
+    // Nothing to persist; the in-memory state this tab already has still
+    // drives this page load, it just will not survive a reload.
+  }
+  window.dispatchEvent(new Event(CHANGE_EVENT))
+}
+
+export const CONSENT_CHANGE_EVENT = CHANGE_EVENT
+export const CONSENT_STORAGE_KEY = STORAGE_KEY
added in remote

@@ -0,0 +1,55 @@
+"use client"
+
+import { Button, Text } from "@medusajs/ui"
+import { useTranslations } from "next-intl"
+import { writeConsent } from "./consent"
+import { useAnalyticsConsent } from "./use-analytics-consent"
+
+/**
+ * The banner analytics has been missing since this storefront gained a GA4
+ * option: consent state, not "the id is set", now gates whether either
+ * counter mounts (t17-analytics step 3). Renders only while consent is
+ * "unknown" â€” a visitor who already answered, in this tab or a previous one,
+ * never sees it again.
+ */
+const AnalyticsConsentBanner = () => {
+  const consent = useAnalyticsConsent()
+  const t = useTranslations("AnalyticsConsent")
+
+  if (consent !== "unknown") {
+    return null
+  }
+
+  return (
+    <div
+      role="region"
+      aria-label={t("message")}
+      className="fixed inset-x-0 bottom-0 z-50 flex flex-col items-center gap-3 border-t border-ui-border-base bg-ui-bg-base p-4 shadow-elevation-flyout small:flex-row small:justify-between"
+      data-testid="analytics-consent-banner"
+    >
+      <Text size="small" className="text-ui-fg-subtle">
+        {t("message")}
+      </Text>
+      <div className="flex shrink-0 gap-2">
+        <Button
+          variant="secondary"
+          size="small"
+          onClick={() => writeConsent("denied")}
+          data-testid="analytics-consent-decline"
+        >
+          {t("decline")}
+        </Button>
+        <Button
+          variant="primary"
+          size="small"
+          onClick={() => writeConsent("granted")}
+          data-testid="analytics-consent-accept"
+        >
+          {t("accept")}
+        </Button>
+      </div>
+    </div>
+  )
+}
+
+export default AnalyticsConsentBanner

@@ -0,0 +1,24 @@
+"use client"
+
+import { useEffect, useState } from "react"
+import { CONSENT_CHANGE_EVENT, readConsent, type ConsentState } from "./consent"
+
+/** Re-renders whenever this tab's or another tab's consent choice changes. */
+export const useAnalyticsConsent = (): ConsentState => {
+  const [state, setState] = useState<ConsentState>("unknown")
+
+  useEffect(() => {
+    setState(readConsent())
+
+    const onChange = () => setState(readConsent())
+    window.addEventListener(CONSENT_CHANGE_EVENT, onChange)
+    window.addEventListener("storage", onChange)
+
+    return () => {
+      window.removeEventListener(CONSENT_CHANGE_EVENT, onChange)
+      window.removeEventListener("storage", onChange)
+    }
+  }, [])
+
+  return state
+}

  their  100644 4f243ceeb4d7c68a9492fff59f813c7a10ea0f85 apps/storefront/src/modules/common/components/analytics-consent/use-idle-after-consent.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,70 @@
+"use client"
+
+import { useEffect, useState } from "react"
+import { useAnalyticsConsent } from "./use-analytics-consent"
+
+const IDLE_TIMEOUT = 3000
+
+const WAKE_EVENTS = [
+  "pointerdown",
+  "keydown",
+  "touchstart",
+  "wheel",
+  "scroll",
+] as const
+
+/**
+ * `true` once the visitor has granted analytics consent AND either gone idle
+ * or interacted with the page â€” the load-deferral strategy both counters
+ * share, so a shop with neither counter costs this storefront nothing on
+ * first paint, and a shop with one costs nothing before consent either
+ * (t17-analytics step 2/3).
+ */
+export const useIdleAfterConsent = (): boolean => {
+  const [shouldLoad, setShouldLoad] = useState(false)
+  const consent = useAnalyticsConsent()
+
+  useEffect(() => {
+    if (consent !== "granted") {
+      return
+    }
+
+    let idleHandle: number | undefined
+    let timeoutHandle: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | undefined
+
+    const stopWaiting = () => {
+      if (idleHandle !== undefined) {
+        window.cancelIdleCallback(idleHandle)
+        idleHandle = undefined
+      }
+
+      if (timeoutHandle !== undefined) {
+        clearTimeout(timeoutHandle)
+        timeoutHandle = undefined
+      }
+
+      for (const event of WAKE_EVENTS) {
+        window.removeEventListener(event, load)
+      }
+    }
+
+    const load = () => {
+      stopWaiting()
+      setShouldLoad(true)
+    }
+
+    for (const event of WAKE_EVENTS) {
+      window.addEventListener(event, load, { once: true, passive: true })
+    }
+
+    if (typeof window.requestIdleCallback === "function") {
+      idleHandle = window.requestIdleCallback(load, { timeout: IDLE_TIMEOUT })
+    } else {
+      timeoutHandle = setTimeout(load, IDLE_TIMEOUT)
+    }
+
+    return stopWaiting
+  }, [consent])
+
+  return shouldLoad
+}

  their  100644 259a51061c46adb25e15350224e1f1bcda7d906c apps/storefront/src/modules/common/components/google-analytics/spa-pageviews.tsx
@@ -0,0 +1,36 @@
+"use client"
+
+import { sendGAEvent } from "@next/third-parties/google"
+import { usePathname, useSearchParams } from "next/navigation"
+import { useEffect, useRef } from "react"
+
+/**
+ * `@next/third-parties`'s `GoogleAnalytics` fires one `page_view` on the
+ * first load and never again (measured against its shipped source, no
+ * `usePathname` anywhere in it) â€” this is an App Router SPA, so every later
+ * navigation is a client-side route change the library never sees. This
+ * effect sends the missed event itself, through `sendGAEvent`, which posts to
+ * the same `dataLayer` the library's own script already set up.
+ */
+const SpaPageviews = () => {
+  const pathname = usePathname()
+  const searchParams = useSearchParams()
+  const isFirstRender = useRef(true)
+
+  useEffect(() => {
+    if (isFirstRender.current) {
+      // The library's own script already sent this one on load.
+      isFirstRender.current = false
+      return
+    }
+
+    const query = searchParams.toString()
+    sendGAEvent("event", "page_view", {
+      page_path: query ? `${pathname}?${query}` : pathname,
+    })
+  }, [pathname, searchParams])
+
+  return null
+}
+
+export default SpaPageviews

  their  100644 f0bc7dd3563bc411fda535ddd198b0906d266d63 apps/storefront/src/modules/common/components/purchase-event/index.tsx
@@ -0,0 +1,130 @@
+"use client"
+
+import { sendGAEvent } from "@next/third-parties/google"
+import { useEffect } from "react"
+import { readConsent } from "@modules/common/components/analytics-consent/consent"
+
+declare global {
+  interface Window {
+    gtag?: (...args: unknown[]) => void
+  }
+}
+
+export type PurchaseEventProps = {
+  orderId: string
+  total: number
+  currencyCode: string
+  ga4MeasurementId: string | null
+  yandexCounterId: number | null
+}
+
+const sentKey = (orderId: string) => `medusa_purchase_sent_${orderId}`
+const POLL_INTERVAL_MS = 200
+const POLL_TIMEOUT_MS = 5000
+
+/**
+ * Waits for a predicate to turn true (the vendor script has attached its
+ * global), polling rather than assuming it is already there: both counters
+ * defer their own script load until idle or a first interaction
+ * (`use-idle-after-consent.ts`), and a customer landing here fresh from
+ * checkout may not have triggered either yet. Gives up silently past the
+ * timeout â€” a purchase event that never fires is a loss the owner can live
+ * with; a page that hangs waiting for it is not.
+ */
+const waitFor = (predicate: () => boolean, onReady: () => void) => {
+  if (predicate()) {
+    onReady()
+    return
+  }
+
+  const start = Date.now()
+  const interval = window.setInterval(() => {
+    if (predicate()) {
+      window.clearInterval(interval)
+      onReady()
+    } else if (Date.now() - start > POLL_TIMEOUT_MS) {
+      window.clearInterval(interval)
+    }
+  }, POLL_INTERVAL_MS)
+}
+
+/**
+ * The e-commerce purchase event (t17-analytics step 4), fired once per order
+ * from the confirmation page, to whichever counters this tenant configured.
+ *
+ * Wrapped so it can never break the page a customer sees right after paying
+ * (the mission's own constraint): every vendor call is inside a `try`, and a
+ * thrown error here is swallowed, not rendered. `sessionStorage` dedupes a
+ * reload of `/order/:id/confirmed` so a refresh does not double-count a sale.
+ */
+const PurchaseEvent = ({
+  orderId,
+  total,
+  currencyCode,
+  ga4MeasurementId,
+  yandexCounterId,
+}: PurchaseEventProps) => {
+  useEffect(() => {
+    if (readConsent() !== "granted") {
+      return
+    }
+
+    if (window.sessionStorage.getItem(sentKey(orderId))) {
+      return
+    }
+
+    const currency = currencyCode.toUpperCase()
+    let firedGa4 = !ga4MeasurementId
+    let firedYandex = !yandexCounterId
+
+    const markSentWhenBothDone = () => {
+      if (firedGa4 && firedYandex) {
+        window.sessionStorage.setItem(sentKey(orderId), "1")
+      }
+    }
+
+    if (ga4MeasurementId) {
+      waitFor(
+        () => typeof window.gtag === "function",
+        () => {
+          try {
+            sendGAEvent("event", "purchase", {
+              transaction_id: orderId,
+              value: total,
+              currency,
+            })
+          } catch (error) {
+            console.error("PurchaseEvent: GA4 call failed; the order page is unaffected", error)
+          } finally {
+            firedGa4 = true
+            markSentWhenBothDone()
+          }
+        }
+      )
+    }
+
+    if (yandexCounterId) {
+      waitFor(
+        () => typeof window.ym === "function",
+        () => {
+          try {
+            window.ym?.(yandexCounterId, "reachGoal", "purchase", {
+              order_id: orderId,
+              value: total,
+              currency,
+            })
+          } catch (error) {
+            console.error("PurchaseEvent: Metrica call failed; the order page is unaffected", error)
+          } finally {
+            firedYandex = true
+            markSentWhenBothDone()
+          }
+        }
+      )
+    }
+  }, [orderId, total, currencyCode, ga4MeasurementId, yandexCounterId])
+
+  return null
+}
+
+export default PurchaseEvent

+"use client"
+
+import Script from "next/script"

+ * non-critical scripts, plus the `noscript` pixel Metrica's own install
+ * instructions ship â€” omitted, Metrica still counts a visit with JavaScript
+ * enabled, but the pixel is what counts one without it.
+ *
+ * Gated on `useIdleAfterConsent`, the same load-deferral `GoogleAnalytics`

+        {`(function(m,e,t,r,i,k,a){m[i]=m[i]||function(){(m[i].a=m[i].a||[]).push(arguments)};
+        m[i].l=1*new Date();
+        for (var j = 0; j < document.scripts.length; j++) {if (document.scripts[j].src === r) { return; }}
+        k=e.createElement(t),a=e.getElementsByTagName(t)[0],k.async=1,k.src=r,a.parentNode.insertBefore(k,a)})
+        (window, document, "script", "https://mc.yandex.ru/metrika/tag.js", "ym");
+
+        ym(${counterId}, "init", {
+             clickmap:true,
+             trackLinks:true,
+             accurateTrackBounce:true
+        });`}
+      </Script>
+      <noscript>
+        <div>
+          <img
+            src={`https://mc.yandex.ru/watch/${counterId}`}
+            style={{ position: "absolute", left: "-9999px" }}
+            alt=""
+          />
+        </div>
+      </noscript>
+      <SpaHits counterId={counterId} />
+    </>
+  )
+}
+
+export default YandexMetrica

@@ -0,0 +1,43 @@
+"use client"
+
+import { usePathname, useSearchParams } from "next/navigation"
+import { useEffect, useRef } from "react"
+
+declare global {
+  interface Window {
+    ym?: (counterId: number, action: string, ...args: unknown[]) => void
+  }
+}
+
+type SpaHitsProps = {
+  counterId: number
+}
+
+/**
+ * Metrica's own snippet counts the first load itself. This is an App Router
+ * SPA: every later navigation is a client-side route change the snippet
+ * never sees, so this effect calls `ym(id, "hit", url)` itself on every
+ * pathname change after the first (the same shape as
+ * `google-analytics/spa-pageviews.tsx`).
+ */
+const SpaHits = ({ counterId }: SpaHitsProps) => {
+  const pathname = usePathname()
+  const searchParams = useSearchParams()
+  const isFirstRender = useRef(true)
+
+  useEffect(() => {
+    if (isFirstRender.current) {
+      isFirstRender.current = false
+      return
+    }
+
+    const query = searchParams.toString()
+    const url = query ? `${pathname}?${query}` : pathname
+
+    window.ym?.(counterId, "hit", url)
+  }, [counterId, pathname, searchParams])
+
+  return null
+}
+
+export default SpaHits

+import { FetchError } from "@medusajs/js-sdk"
+import {
+  QueryKey,
+  UseMutationOptions,
+  UseQueryOptions,
+  useMutation,
+  useQuery,
+} from "@tanstack/react-query"
+

+import { queryClient } from "../../lib/query-client"
+
+// tenant-panel: own hook, no Admin counterpart â€” GET/PUT /tenants/analytics
+// per t17-analytics step 1. Same shape as hooks/api/locale-settings.tsx.
+
+export type TenantAnalyticsSettings = {
+  yandex_metrica_id: string | null
+  ga4_measurement_id: string | null
+}
+
+export type TenantAnalyticsResponse = { analytics: TenantAnalyticsSettings }
+
+const ANALYTICS_QUERY_KEY = "tenant-analytics" as const
+export const tenantAnalyticsQueryKeys = queryKeysFactory(ANALYTICS_QUERY_KEY)
+
+export const useTenantAnalytics = (
+  options?: Omit<
+    UseQueryOptions<TenantAnalyticsResponse, FetchError, TenantAnalyticsResponse, QueryKey>,
+    "queryFn" | "queryKey"
+  >
+) => {
+  const { data, ...rest } = useQuery({
+    queryFn: () => sdk.client.fetch<TenantAnalyticsResponse>("/tenants/analytics"),
+    queryKey: tenantAnalyticsQueryKeys.details(),
+    ...options,
+  })
+
+  return { ...data, ...rest }
+}
+
+export const useUpdateTenantAnalytics = (
+  options?: UseMutationOptions<TenantAnalyticsResponse, FetchError, TenantAnalyticsSettings>
+) => {
+  return useMutation({
+    mutationFn: (payload) =>
+      sdk.client.fetch<TenantAnalyticsResponse>("/tenants/analytics", {
+        method: "PUT",
+        body: payload,
+      }),
+    onSuccess: (data, variables, context) => {
+      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: tenantAnalyticsQueryKeys.details() })
+      options?.onSuccess?.(data, variables, context)
+    },
+    ...options,
+  })
+}

@@ -0,0 +1,52 @@
+import { Text } from "@medusajs/ui"
+import { useTranslation } from "react-i18next"
+
+type SalesSummaryChartProps = {
+  daily: { date: string; revenue: number; order_count: number }[]
+}
+
+/**
+ * A plain CSS bar chart, no charting library: 30 bars at most, one number
+ * each, and the repository has none installed (rule 32a â€” YAGNI, this does
+ * not need one). Every bar is real data from the backend, height zero
+ * included â€” never invented (step 6).
+ */
+export const SalesSummaryChart = ({ daily }: SalesSummaryChartProps) => {
+  const { t, i18n } = useTranslation()
+  const max = Math.max(1, ...daily.map((day) => day.revenue))
+
+  return (
+    <div className="flex flex-col gap-y-2 rounded-lg border p-4">
+      <Text size="small" weight="plus">
+        {t("salesSummary.chartTitle")}
+      </Text>
+      {/* `items-stretch` (the default, stated explicitly): each bar's
+          percentage height resolves against ITS OWN parent, so that parent
+          must actually BE h-32 tall â€” `items-end` here would size it to its
+          content instead and every percentage height would resolve against
+          zero (measured: every bar invisible until this was fixed). */}
+      <div className="flex h-32 items-stretch gap-1" data-testid="sales-summary-chart">
+        {daily.map((day) => {
+          const heightPct = Math.round((day.revenue / max) * 100)
+          const label = new Date(`${day.date}T00:00:00Z`).toLocaleDateString(i18n.language, {
+            day: "numeric",
+            month: "short",
+          })
+
+          return (
+            <div
+              key={day.date}
+              className="group relative flex flex-1 flex-col justify-end"
+              title={`${label}: ${day.revenue}`}
+            >
+              <div
+                className="w-full rounded-t bg-ui-tag-blue-icon"
+                style={{ height: `${Math.max(heightPct, day.revenue > 0 ? 2 : 0)}%` }}
+              />
+            </div>
+          )
+        })}
+      </div>
+    </div>
+  )
+}

@@ -0,0 +1,71 @@
+import { Text } from "@medusajs/ui"
+import { useTranslation } from "react-i18next"
+import { getStylizedAmount } from "../../lib/money-amount-helpers"
+import type { AnalyticsAggregate } from "../../hooks/api/analytics-summary"
+
+type SalesSummaryMetricsProps = {
+  current: AnalyticsAggregate
+  change: { revenue_pct: number | null; order_count_pct: number | null; aov_pct: number | null }
+  currencyCode: string
+}
+
+const ChangeBadge = ({ pct }: { pct: number | null }) => {
+  if (pct === null) {
+    return null
+  }
+
+  const positive = pct >= 0
+  return (
+    <Text
+      size="xsmall"
+      className={positive ? "text-ui-tag-green-text" : "text-ui-tag-red-text"}
+    >
+      {positive ? "+" : ""}
+      {pct}%
+    </Text>
+  )
+}
+
+/** Revenue, order count and AOV for the current period, each with the change against the previous one. */
+export const SalesSummaryMetrics = ({ current, change, currencyCode }: SalesSummaryMetricsProps) => {
+  const { t } = useTranslation()
+
+  const cards = [
+    {
+      key: "revenue",
+      label: t("salesSummary.revenue"),
+      value: getStylizedAmount(current.revenue, currencyCode),
+      pct: change.revenue_pct,
+    },
+    {
+      key: "orders",
+      label: t("salesSummary.orders"),
+      value: String(current.order_count),
+      pct: change.order_count_pct,
+    },
+    {
+      key: "aov",
+      label: t("salesSummary.aov"),
+      value: getStylizedAmount(current.aov, currencyCode),
+      pct: change.aov_pct,
+    },
+  ]
+
+  return (
+    <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-4 md:grid-cols-3">
+      {cards.map((card) => (
+        <div key={card.key} className="flex flex-col gap-y-1 rounded-lg border p-4">
+          <Text size="small" className="text-ui-fg-subtle">
+            {card.label}
+          </Text>
+          <div className="flex items-baseline gap-x-2">
+            <Text size="large" weight="plus">
+              {card.value}
+            </Text>
+            <ChangeBadge pct={card.pct} />
+          </div>
+        </div>
+      ))}
+    </div>
+  )
+}

+import { Text } from "@medusajs/ui"

+import { getStylizedAmount } from "../../lib/money-amount-helpers"
+
+type SalesSummaryTopProductsProps = {

+  currencyCode: string
+}

+/** The five best-selling products of the current period, by revenue. */
+export const SalesSummaryTopProducts = ({ products, currencyCode }: SalesSummaryTopProductsProps) => {
+  const { t } = useTranslation()
+
+  return (
+    <div className="flex flex-col gap-y-2 rounded-lg border p-4">

+        <Text size="small" className="text-ui-fg-subtle">
+          {t("salesSummary.noSalesThisPeriod")}
+        </Text>
+      ) : (
+        <ul className="flex flex-col gap-y-2">

+                {product.title}
+              </Text>

+                {t("salesSummary.topProductRow", {
+                  quantity: product.quantity,
+                  revenue: getStylizedAmount(product.revenue, currencyCode),
+                })}
+              </Text>
+            </li>
+          ))}
+        </ul>
+      )}
+    </div>
+  )

@@ -0,0 +1,73 @@
+import { useState } from "react"
+import { Button, Heading, Text } from "@medusajs/ui"
+import { useTranslation } from "react-i18next"
+import { useTenantAnalyticsSummary } from "../../hooks/api/analytics-summary"
+import type { AnalyticsPeriodKey } from "../../hooks/api/analytics-summary"
+import { SalesSummaryMetrics } from "./sales-summary-metrics"
+import { SalesSummaryChart } from "./sales-summary-chart"
+import { SalesSummaryTopProducts } from "./sales-summary-top-products"
+
+const PERIODS: AnalyticsPeriodKey[] = ["today", "7d", "30d"]
+
+/**
+ * t17-analytics step 5/6: revenue, order count, AOV, a daily chart and the
+ * top five products for a period the owner picks. Three states, not two
+ * (rule 19h): `isPending` (not answered yet â€” real, disabled period buttons,
+ * no numbers guessed), `has_orders: false` (this shop has never had an
+ * order â€” the honest "Ğ·Ğ°ĞºĞ°Ğ·Ğ¾Ğ² Ğ¿Ğ¾ĞºĞ° Ğ½ĞµÑ‚"), and the computed summary, whose
+ * own current period may legitimately be all zeros.
+ */
+export const SalesSummary = () => {
+  const { t } = useTranslation()
+  const [period, setPeriod] = useState<AnalyticsPeriodKey>("7d")
+  const { data, isPending, isError, error } = useTenantAnalyticsSummary({ period })
+
+  if (isError) {
+    throw error
+  }
+
+  return (
+    <div className="flex flex-col gap-y-4 border-t pt-6">
+      <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
+        <Heading level="h2">{t("salesSummary.title")}</Heading>
+        <div className="flex gap-x-1">
+          {PERIODS.map((key) => (
+            <Button
+              key={key}
+              size="small"
+              variant={period === key ? "primary" : "secondary"}
+              disabled={isPending}
+              onClick={() => setPeriod(key)}
+              data-testid={`sales-summary-period-${key}`}
+            >
+              {t(`salesSummary.period.${key}`)}
+            </Button>
+          ))}
+        </div>
+      </div>
+
+      {isPending ? (
+        <Text size="small" className="text-ui-fg-subtle" data-testid="sales-summary-loading">
+          {t("salesSummary.loading")}
+        </Text>
+      ) : data && data.has_orders === false ? (
+        <Text size="small" className="text-ui-fg-subtle" data-testid="sales-summary-empty">
+          {t("salesSummary.noOrdersYet")}
+        </Text>
+      ) : data && data.has_orders ? (
+        <>
+          <SalesSummaryMetrics
+            current={data.current}
+            change={data.change}
+            currencyCode={data.currency_code ?? "usd"}
+          />
+          <SalesSummaryChart daily={data.daily} />
+          <SalesSummaryTopProducts
+            products={data.top_products}
+            currencyCode={data.currency_code ?? "usd"}
+          />
+        </>
+      ) : null}
+    </div>
+  )
+}

@@ -0,0 +1,129 @@
+import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react"
+import { Button, Container, Heading, Hint, Input, Label, Text, toast } from "@medusajs/ui"
+import { useTranslation } from "react-i18next"
+
+import { SingleColumnPage } from "../../../components/layout/pages"
+import {
+  useTenantAnalytics,
+  useUpdateTenantAnalytics,
+} from "../../../hooks/api/analytics-settings"
+
+// t17-analytics step 1: the shop's own Yandex Metrica / GA4 ids. Same
+// rule-19h shape as ../notifications/notifications.tsx: real, disabled
+// controls while the record loads, and `touchedRef` so a slow answer never
+// overwrites what the owner already typed.
+
+type Draft = { yandex_metrica_id: string; ga4_measurement_id: string }
+
+const EMPTY_DRAFT: Draft = { yandex_metrica_id: "", ga4_measurement_id: "" }
+
+export const AnalyticsSettings = () => {
+  const { t } = useTranslation()
+  const { analytics, isPending, isError, error } = useTenantAnalytics()
+
+  const [loaded, setLoaded] = useState<boolean | null>(null)
+  const [draft, setDraft] = useState<Draft>(EMPTY_DRAFT)
+  const touchedRef = useRef(false)
+
+  useEffect(() => {
+    if (isPending) {
+      return
+    }
+    setLoaded(true)
+    if (!touchedRef.current) {
+      setDraft({
+        yandex_metrica_id: analytics?.yandex_metrica_id ?? "",
+        ga4_measurement_id: analytics?.ga4_measurement_id ?? "",
+      })
+    }
+  }, [isPending, analytics])
+
+  const { mutateAsync: save, isPending: isSaving } = useUpdateTenantAnalytics()
+
+  if (isError) {
+    throw error
+  }
+
+  const busy = loaded === null || isSaving
+
+  const update = <K extends keyof Draft>(key: K, value: Draft[K]) => {
+    touchedRef.current = true
+    setDraft((previous) => ({ ...previous, [key]: value }))
+  }
+
+  const handleSubmit = async (event: React.FormEvent) => {
+    event.preventDefault()
+    await save(
+      {
+        yandex_metrica_id: draft.yandex_metrica_id.trim(),
+        ga4_measurement_id: draft.ga4_measurement_id.trim(),
+      },
+      {
+        onSuccess: (response) => {
+          touchedRef.current = false
+          setDraft({
+            yandex_metrica_id: response.analytics.yandex_metrica_id ?? "",
+            ga4_measurement_id: response.analytics.ga4_measurement_id ?? "",
+          })
+          toast.success(t("analyticsSettings.saved"))
+        },
+        onError: (saveError) => toast.error(saveError.message),
+      }
+    )
+  }
+
+  return (
+    <SingleColumnPage hasOutlet={false} widgets={{ before: [], after: [] }}>
+      <Container className="divide-y p-0">
+        <div className="px-6 py-4">
+          <Heading>{t("analyticsSettings.title")}</Heading>
+          <Text className="text-ui-fg-subtle" size="small">
+            {t("analyticsSettings.subtitle")}
+          </Text>
+        </div>
+
+        <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="flex flex-col gap-y-5 px-6 py-6">
+          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-4 md:grid-cols-2">
+            <div className="flex flex-col gap-y-2">
+              <Label htmlFor="yandex_metrica_id">
+                {t("analyticsSettings.yandexMetrica.label")}
+              </Label>
+              <Input
+                id="yandex_metrica_id"
+                name="yandex_metrica_id"
+                autoComplete="off"
+                inputMode="numeric"
+                value={draft.yandex_metrica_id}
+                disabled={busy}
+                onChange={(event) => update("yandex_metrica_id", event.target.value)}
+                placeholder={loaded === null ? t("analyticsSettings.loading") : "12345678"}
+              />
+              <Hint>{t("analyticsSettings.yandexMetrica.hint")}</Hint>
+            </div>
+            <div className="flex flex-col gap-y-2">
+              <Label htmlFor="ga4_measurement_id">
+                {t("analyticsSettings.ga4.label")}
+              </Label>
+              <Input
+                id="ga4_measurement_id"
+                name="ga4_measurement_id"
+                autoComplete="off"
+                value={draft.ga4_measurement_id}
+                disabled={busy}
+                onChange={(event) => update("ga4_measurement_id", event.target.value.toUpperCase())}
+                placeholder={loaded === null ? t("analyticsSettings.loading") : "G-XXXXXXXXXX"}
+              />
+              <Hint>{t("analyticsSettings.ga4.hint")}</Hint>
+            </div>
+          </div>
+
+          <div>
+            <Button type="submit" size="small" isLoading={isSaving} disabled={busy}>
+              {t("analyticsSettings.save")}
+            </Button>
+          </div>
+        </form>
+      </Container>
+    </SingleColumnPage>
+  )
+}

+- `GET /storefront/by-host` Ğ¾Ñ‚Ğ´Ğ°Ñ‘Ñ‚ `analytics` Ğ´Ğ»Ñ Ğ²Ğ¸Ñ‚Ñ€Ğ¸Ğ½Ñ‹ (ÑˆĞ°Ğ³ 1).
